Public halving gives two group points; the canonical interval bound selects the correct branch.

The interval-divisor construction is now implemented. K contains all nonzero subgroup x coordinates; V^2=X^3+aX+b mod K chooses canonical lower-half y signs. There are 2^m root orientations, and changing P to 2P preserves K but changes V. The reference enumerates m points and has linear-size expanded evaluation. For div(F_N)=sum_(j=1..N)(jP)-(S_N)-(N-1)(O), the ratio F_N(T-P)/F_N(T)=C_N*g_(S_N,P)(T)/f_(N+1,P)(T) follows from exact divisor cancellation. Handle S_N separately when its intended zero cancels. No universal circuit lower bound follows.

The interval normalization task is implemented. Use s=N(N+1)/2 as an integer, not a canonical reduced index in W. The ordinary constant telescopes from normalized line quotients at -P. The vertical case uses the invariant derivative and a pole coefficient; its simple-zero condition follows in the stated range. The sparse valuation difference can be evaluated at public known points, but its cumulative pattern is still the desired interval membership. Three unsampled strict charts are (p,r,N)=(5,7,3),(7,13,6),(17,19,9); preserve this limit.

The interval transpose task is implemented. R_(N,L)=A*R_(L,N)*g_(S_N,LP)/g_(S_L,NP)*f_(L,NP)/f_(N,LP). The ordinary constant A=W(s)W(t+N)W(N)^(L-1)/(W(s+L)W(t)W(L)^(N-1)) has no factorial product after cancellation. At vanishing shifted indices replace W(a) by -D psi_a(P)/a. This gives shorter-dimension reference evaluation plus logarithmic correction, not a fast diagonal evaluator. The norm F_N(T)F_N(-T)=(-1)^(N-1)K_N(x)/(x-x(S_N)) retains both orientations.

The norm-factor task is implemented. H=(x-x(S))*F=A+yB lies in L((m+1)O) of dimension m+1, with A^2-fB^2=(-1)^(m-1)K(x)(x-x(S)). Same-sum sign flips are zero-sum subsets of 1..m modulo r; they yield distinct normalized factors with identical norm and pole restrictions. The canonical translation equation distinguishes them: equal translated divisor differences force equal divisors in this supported class, then normalization fixes the function. This is uniqueness, not fast construction. The reference Gaussian systems use supplied oriented zeros, not the public translation operator.

The public translation operator is assembled and solved. It expands L(H)=H(T-P)-R(T)*(x(T-P)-x(S))/(x(T)-x(S))*H(T) on L((m+1)O), clears nonzero rational denominators and equates both polynomial numerator components. A global divisor argument gives a one-dimensional kernel in the entire bounded space: the ratio to the canonical factor is translation-invariant, has possible poles only on H, and no pole at O. The constructor never reads oriented roots; those are used only afterward as a reference. Dense matrix storage is O(r^2), standard elimination O(r^3), and expanded output/query O(r).

Next candidate: exploit rational substitution/multiplication to apply the operator without storing the dense matrix. Audit whether a structured nullspace algorithm or direct point functional avoids building O(r) coefficients, with full construction/query accounting. Measure both storage and arithmetic work for the matrix-free implementation, including construction and queries. Preserve prior chart omissions and source restrictions.

The wider portfolio remains open: efficient inverse representations, an absolute recurrence anchor, efficient marked-map synthesis from Q, compatible characteristic-changing transport with an allowed extractor, and cheap compressed spectral evaluation. Existing restrictions are scoped, not universal impossibility claims. Choose encoding experiments to test a specific structural hypothesis about inverse cost.

Completion requires an explicit general algorithm, correct unknown-scalar recovery, and an accounted setup/query/space/precision advantage over relevant general methods at the claimed scope. The goal is not complete or externally blocked. Preserve all user exclusions and full objective across continuations.

Standard negation symmetry, not an attack or proved smaller circuit. ## Prime-dilation sparsity bound

See [sparse-dilation-bound/REPORT.md](sparse-dilation-bound/REPORT.md) and its [two-page PDF](sparse-dilation-bound/report.pdf). For the nonzero centered-y scalar polynomial over F_r, t nonzero monomials imply r-1 <= 3 sum(l_j^2) (2 floor(N(p-1)/(2r))+1), N=bitlength(r)*t, with the first N distinct primes excluding p and r. Full prime-order groups therefore require t=Omega(r^(1/4)/(log r)^(3/2)). This excludes polylog monomial sparsity for this feature, including high-degree sparse polynomials; it is not a circuit-size or general ECDLP lower bound. Exact checks: six historical consistency/cardinality records, two signed moment controls and 270 dilated label-monomial identities. No relation search, new curve trials or recoveries. Remaining direction: explicit short nonlinear circuits for a directly invertible public feature or character projection, with complete evaluation/inversion/precision accounting.


## Compact unweighted blocks and public slope feature

[Report](compact-unweighted-block/REPORT.md), [PDF](compact-unweighted-block/report.pdf), [checker](compact-unweighted-block/check.py). Exact S_n(T)=g_(nP)(T+(n-1)P)+C_n with C_(m+n)=C_m+C_n-slope(mP,nP). A binary chain evaluates the constant and endpoint in O(log n) field operations, closing the earlier weighted-blocks omitted-constant gap for unweighted sums. Full trace K(Q)=C_(r-1)(Q); public lambda(Q)=K(Q)/r is O(log r) and obeys lambda(A+B)=lambda(A)+lambda(B)+slope(A,B) for nonzero A,B,A+B. Scalar multiplication still requires unknown-index C_n, so no inversion supplied; a nonzero additive F_p homomorphism is impossible for p!=r. Checks: 1024 block identities, 26 chain counts, 124 full constants, 81 merges, 162 feature additions, 188 feature multiplications; four weighted counterexamples. No timing, spectral measurement or recovery. Next: relate lambda to division-polynomial jet normalization and seek joint inversion with full accounting.


## Slope-chain / second-jet equivalence

[Report](slope-jet-equivalence/REPORT.md), [PDF](slope-jet-equivalence/report.pdf), [implementation](slope-jet-equivalence/extractor.py). The compact public feature lambda=-U exactly: C_n=U(nP)-nU(P), C_(r-1)=-rU(P). Thus it supplies an alternative O(log r) chain evaluator of U, with constant live field/point state, but no independent equation. Given the MISSING minimal-index slope anchor C_t(R), the conditional extractor obtains t mod p=(U(Q)-C_t)/U(R) and uses at most two replays after a public choice among P,2P,3P. This is a different oracle from L_t=-tC_t; do not claim an attack speedup or divide by unknown t, especially t divisible by p. Checks: 46 jet/chain identities, 46 L_t identities, 46 supplied-anchor controls and wrong-anchor rejections, seven p-divisible indices, five rebased cases, one identity target. Zero public-input recoveries. Next candidate: inverse-circuit encoding of the slope-chain state, requiring a frozen matched public-target comparison before any performance claim.


## Frozen slope-state inverse diagnostic

[Report](slope-inverse/reports/run-20261030T0445Z/REPORT.md), [PDF](slope-inverse/reports/run-20261030T0445Z/report.pdf), [export](slope-inverse/runs/finite/export.json), [soundness](slope-inverse/THEORY.md). Protocol 774e9b84dea7f4330deea8438cf449b8f0675335021d32ab35dc5518fdd9541f frozen before 48 trials. Eight public synthetic targets, six arms, rlimit300000, seed1729. Verified SAT: reference2/8, baseline2/8, candidate4/8, renamedAA3/8; supplied-scalar positive7/8; negativeUNSAT2/8. Remaining28 rows unknown, canceled at resource300000; no process failures. A/A mismatch on p31 index21, positive unresolved p61 index71, six negative unknown: preregistered control requirements unmet, comparison incomplete, no improvement conclusion. Candidate adds public C_k=U(Q)-kU(P) and state recurrence; unknown scalar never supplied except positive. Candidate public setup6..9 correctioncalls24..63 chainsteps, no timing/memory speed claim. Export file audit0findings does not cure controls. No rerun/tuning. Next isolate variable-name/branching nuisance with a new frozen factorial design and controls that complete; do not infer general ECDLP advantage from raw4vs2.


## Canonical slope circuits, crossed seeds

[Report](slope-canonical/reports/run-20261030T0454Z/REPORT.md), [PDF](slope-canonical/reports/run-20261030T0454Z/report.pdf), [export](slope-canonical/runs/finite/export.json). Frozen protocol5877f518a35a809457eff4a42054b7849a85b65e5ac45c3a0becb178c10b4e32, source5874b5a, freeze2bae8fa, raw a25c668. All144 rows complete:59 SAT verified,20 UNSAT,65 unknown canceled at300000. All32 AA pairs identical in canonical query hash,status,resource count (baseline and candidate names normalized then fresh-context parsed). Fully specified positive16/16 and negative16/16 pass; privileged internal traces, not public recovery. Original scalar-only positive14/16 and unrestricted inconsistent4/16 UNSAT with12unknown retained as diagnostics. Eight unique public targets reused at two seeds: baseline/candidate2/4 at1729,3/3 at2718. Atp17 index9 seed flips which arm solves; atp17 index17 candidate solves both seeds, baseline neither. No p31/p61 main recovery. Do not pool repeated-target7vs5 into population success. Exportaudit0findings, PDF2pages inspected. Prefix nuisance removed in finite panel; no seed-uniform count advantage, no scaling/runtime claim. Canonical pipeline is a sounder comparison instrument, not a new scalar equation. Next work should require a materially different inversion mechanism or a sharper structure-specific propagation argument, rather than another untargeted seed sweep.


## Kernel-polynomial readout of U

[Report](kernel-jet-readout/REPORT.md), [PDF](kernel-jet-readout/report.pdf), [checker](kernel-jet-readout/check.py). For the monic subgroup kernel K of degree(r-1)/2, rU(Q)=D²K(Q)/(2DK(Q))=Fprime(x)/(2y)+yKsecond(x)/Kprime(x). Proof uses L=DK/K and L(T+A)-L(T)=-r g_(-A)(T)+a_A; trace identifies a_A=rU(A). Full prime-order groups admit curve-only K=gcd(X^p-X,F^((p-1)/2)-1), but the expanded gcd is not logarithmic-cost. Proper F11 subgroup order5 has K degree2 vs full gcd degree7. Checks154 simple roots/readouts vs jets and chain, five full gcds, one proper-subgroup mismatch, forty exterior translation identities, six ambient X^p-X shortcut counterexamples. Zero recovery/new curves/random trials. Lower polynomial degree is not cheaper evaluation; existing chain is already O(log r), while this reference stores Theta(r) coefficients. New equation is same U, not independent. Next construction must retain marking or another invertible scalar signal with explicit setup cost, not merely optimize the unmarked kernel representation.


## Compact selected kernel derivatives from Miller values

[Report](miller-logjet-closure/REPORT.md), [PDF](miller-logjet-closure/report.pdf), [public evaluator](miller-logjet-closure/kernel_jets.py). For Q nonzero order-r, A=Q/2: DK(Q)=-f_(r,A)(Q), Kprime=-f/(2y), Ksecond=Kprime*(rU-Fprime/(2y))/y. Proof: normalized f_(r,A)(T)=DK(-A)K(T)/K(T-A), then take limit T=2A. Public evaluator uses known halving, local Miller valuation/leading coefficient machinery and public U, all O(log r) field/group ops; does NOT construct K or use Q scalar label. 154 first and154 second derivatives match expanded kernels on six retained curves, including proper subgroup. This makes the divisor-character residue denominator 2yKprime compact; weighted numerator and allowed destination extraction remain unresolved. Also Dlog f=r(g_A+U(A)); all ordinary derivative ratios obey T_(n+1)=D T_n+G T_n. Six exact first/second/third identities, six wrongconstants,148 regular evaluations pass. Frobenius factor1+(x-xQ)^p has D=0 and value1 atQ: all raw ordinary jets agree but degree-p Hasse coefficient differs byf(Q); six seriescontrols24 finitejets checked. This altered function changes divisor, not a second normalized Miller function. No recovery/newcurves/random/performance claim. Next focus compact marked numerator or useful high-order coefficient with explicit cost, not more ordinary derivatives.


## Kernel-phase equivalence and compact restricted character

[Report](kernel-phase-equivalence/REPORT.md), [PDF](kernel-phase-equivalence/report.pdf), [public evaluator](kernel-phase-equivalence/miller_phase.py). General multiplier law e(nQ)=e(Q)^(n^2)/psi_n(Q)^r, e=-DK, includes p-divisible n. In the coprime domain DK=-c^(-r): selected kernel derivatives contain no independent equation beyond c,U and coordinates. When r divides p-1, q(Q)=e(Q)^((p-1)/r), q(O)=1 gives chi_P(Q)=q(Q+P)/(q(Q)q(P))=lambda^k, lambda=q(P)^2. Public O(log r+log p) field/group evaluation uses local Miller values, no expanded kernel, marked numerator or scalar labels. All four retained proper-subgroup fixtures calibrate nontrivially; no universal nontriviality claimed. Checks:770 multiplier identities (166 p-divisible),150 phase equivalences/alternate evaluations/second-derivative reconstructions/wrong-sign controls,30 quadratic and30 linear character values,244 addition pairs,2 domain rejections. No destination logarithm extractor, general curve coverage, timing or public scalar recovery. Two PDF pages inspected. Next obligation: compatible scalar-preserving transport outside r dividing p-1 with explicit representation and an allowed extractor.


## Character output-field extension and descent obstruction

[Report](character-extension-obstruction/REPORT.md), [PDF](character-extension-obstruction/report.pdf), [checker](character-extension-obstruction/check.py). For r not dividing p-1 and m=ord_r(p)>1, embedding e(Q)=-DK(Q) in F_(p^d), m|d, then raising to (p^d-1)/r gives1. Any Frobenius-equivariant scalar map from rational H into mu_r is trivial. A nontrivial explicit F_p linear representation has dimension at least m, by the primitive eigenvalue minimal polynomial; this is NOT a compressed-circuit lower bound. Even a supplied primitive character has norm1 and trace invariant under k->pk; for m=r-1 its trace is -1 for every nonzero k. Nine retained fixtures,348 base-field and450 kernel-value collapses,185 formal trace identities,155 norm exponents,18 constant-trace controls. No new curves, statistical measurements or recoveries. Formal trace polynomials in F_p[Z]/Phi_r are identity certificates, not public Q evaluators. Two PDF pages inspected. Next: non-rational marking or different destination with explicit orientation-preserving permitted extractor; field inclusion, norm or one trace alone cannot supply it.


## Marked primitive trace scalar anchor

[Report](trace-window-reconstruction/REPORT.md), [PDF](trace-window-reconstruction/report.pdf), [conditional decoder](trace-window-reconstruction/finite.py). For primitive z of order r in F_(p^m), m=ord_r(p), and Frobenius coset representatives U, A(w)=sum_u Tr(w^u/(1-z^u)). Exactly A(z^k)=k-(r+1)/2 mod p for 1<=k<r, A(1)=(r-1)/2. Even half norms are constant and odd rational half norms give the same calibrated character. Composed extraction recovers the same185 inputs (155 singleton,30 zero), nine one-query controls unresolved;932 queries,747 shift additions,22788 orbit additions,23690 reads,24592 field multiplications. Old generator/source compilers, reference scalar multiplication/point enumeration, gcd and subset compiler guarded during extraction. Nine list-point controls pass. No new curve, blind/large recovery or timing. The source construction uses linear work; destination extraction is the next cost to reduce. Next: genuinely fast signed half norm, not its constant square or arbitrary root. Both PDF pages inspected.
